- **discriminating observation:** Obtain dated transaction-price distributions and designs separating attributes in specified tasks and populations.
- **evidence sought found:** Compared case-level claims DIG-K24, DIG-K09, DIG-K18. Inspected surveys describe device use/importance and task perceptions, not attribute intervention effects or the required price distributions.
- **remaining ambiguity:** Unestablished is not false. No current global VR failure or device-price verdict is claimed.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis; targeted revision after independent first-round criticism

**Counterevidence/rival:** Prices, tariffs, distribution, software, network reach, ergonomics and constrained alternatives can accompany or substitute for portability.

**Inference limit:** Unestablished is not false. No current global VR failure or device-price verdict is claimed.

## SYN-K19

Production capability, low cost and local adoption require distinct observations. Procurement and production knowledge appear in bounded capability-building histories, but modeled scale/R&D/learning shares depend on definitions and do not identify a universal pathway.

**Cases:** ENE-C01; ENE-C03; ENE-C04; SKL-C03. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** hypothesis.

- [ENE-S01: Beyond the learning curve: factors influencing cost reductions in photovoltaics](https://wikis.mit.edu/confluence/download/attachments/21139745/Nemet_BeyondtheLearningCurve_EP_06.pdf?api=v2), Table 3 and §4.3, p. 3227. Supports: Bounded evidence in ENE-K02: 43% plant-size allocation and semiconductor silicon-supply spillover.
- [ENE-S02: Evaluating the causes of cost reduction in photovoltaic modules](https://static1.squarespace.com/static/56afb881e321406be6160228/t/5bff10822b6a2884d7267f17/1543442566890/Evaluating%2Bthe%2Bcauses%2Bof%2Bcost%2Breduction%2Bin%2Bphotovoltaic%2Bmodules.pdf), §§4.1–4.2, Table 3, pp. 705–707. Supports: Bounded evidence in ENE-K02: 8% and 36% plant-size allocations; revised cost equation; semiconductor supply/know-how before the mid-2000s transition.
- [ENE-S03: Innovation and international technology transfer: The case of the Chinese photovoltaic industry (CERNA Working Paper 2010-12; HAL version 1)](https://minesparis-psl.hal.science/hal-00498578v1/document), Abstract and §1.1, working-paper pp. 1, 4–5. Supports: Bounded evidence in ENE-K03: Production/export estimates and separate 2009 domestic-demand share.
- [ENE-S03: Innovation and international technology transfer: The case of the Chinese photovoltaic industry (CERNA Working Paper 2010-12; HAL version 1)](https://minesparis-psl.hal.science/hal-00498578v1/document), §§1.2, 2.1–2.3, working-paper pp. 7–11; conclusion pp. 17–18. Supports: Bounded evidence in ENE-K04: Turnkey lines, vendor training, diaspora recruitment and segment-specific entry barriers.
- [ENE-S08: Computers in Spaceflight: The NASA Experience — Chapter 2, Evolution of the hardware](https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/static/history/computers/Ch2-4.html), Printed pp. 33–34; notes 30–35. Supports: Bounded evidence in ENE-K10: Single-gate choice, initially higher cost, large 1963 production share and participant disagreement over perceived risk.
- [ENE-S09: Learning-by-Doing Spillovers in the Semiconductor Industry](https://gwern.net/doc/economics/experience-curve/1994-irwin.pdf), §IV.D and conclusion, pp. 1223–1224, Table 7. Supports: Bounded evidence in ENE-K12: Weak cross-generation effects and entry/exit examples.
- [ENE-S11: Determinants of lithium-ion battery technology cost decline](https://pubs.rsc.org/en/content/articlepdf/2021/ee/d1ee01313k), pp. 6088–6091, Figures 7–9. Supports: Bounded evidence in ENE-K15: Definitions, baseline shares, alternative-assignment sensitivity and proprietary information limits.
- [SKL-S07: John H Hall](https://home.nps.gov/hafe/learn/historyculture/john-h-hall.htm), Contract paragraph and 1822/1832 testimony. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K07: Separates patent, procurement, tooling and contemporaries' assertions about ordinary labour/machinery.
- [SKL-S06: Archeological Investigation of the Armory Street, Lower Armory Grounds, Harpers Ferry Armory (46JF518)](https://npshistory.com/series/archeology/ncr/21/report.pdf), p. 55. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K07: Main factory's piece-rate and 55-task organisation already existed by 1816.
- [SKL-S06: Archeological Investigation of the Armory Street, Lower Armory Grounds, Harpers Ferry Armory (46JF518)](https://npshistory.com/series/archeology/ncr/21/report.pdf), p. 59, notes 244–245. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K08: 1823 gauge adoption and separate gauges to check working gauges.
- [SKL-S12: Box of U.S. Rifle Model 1841 Inspection Gauges (View 4), HAFE 7427](https://artsandculture.google.com/asset/view-4/KAFQGvXgiHqy_Q?childAssetId=XgHi054cXIxrog), Catalog HAFE7427, ca.1846. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K08: Surviving Model 1841 inspection set attributed to Thornton; same set as S06 figure 19.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** underdetermined
- **mechanism chain:** Specified buyers, tooling, skilled transfer or process development -> potential reliable production -> possible market access; deployment and capture require separate links.
- **strongest rival:** Demand selection, imported equipment, material prices, redesign and model attribution rules can explain the same cost/output trends; cumulative output may be consequence as well as cause.
- **discriminating observation:** Trace procurement/tooling/transfer before reliable output; compare local production with local use; test model sensitivity and cross-generation transfer.
- **evidence sought found:** Compared case-level claims ENE-K02, ENE-K03, ENE-K04, ENE-K10, ENE-K12, ENE-K15, SKL-K07, SKL-K08. Chinese PV production/export contrast separates manufacture from domestic use; PV decompositions disagree on plant-size shares; DRAM learning is not reliably inherited across all generations; Hall’s gauges preserve measurement work.
- **remaining ambiguity:** These are linked but different mechanisms, not independent estimates of one learning effect. No cost percentage is transportable without its model and period.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis; targeted revision after independent first-round criticism

**Counterevidence/rival:** Demand selection, imported equipment, material prices, redesign and model attribution rules can explain the same cost/output trends; cumulative output may be consequence as well as cause.

**Inference limit:** These are linked but different mechanisms, not independent estimates of one learning effect. No cost percentage is transportable without its model and period.

## SYN-K20

Measured behaviour supports local intervention and expressed-meaning claims more strongly than universal psychological labels. Offer timing, summaries, communication incentives and bicycle packages have bounded effects; present bias, attention, reinforcement and psychological ownership remain incompletely separated from realistic rivals.

**Cases:** PSY-C01; PSY-C02; PSY-C03; PSY-C04; PSY-C05. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** descriptive.

- [PSY-S01: Nudging Farmers to Use Fertilizer: Theory and Experimental Evidence from Kenya](https://www.atai-research.org/wp-content/uploads/2016/07/aer-Nudging-Fertilizer-2011.pdf), pp.2375–2377, §VI pp.2383–2387; Table6. Supports: Bounded evidence in PSY-K03: Timing arms, prechoice, maize-purchase test, pilot intention/purchase gaps and alternative explanations.
- [PSY-S03: Learning Through Noticing: Theory and Evidence from a Field Experiment](https://www.hbs.edu/ris/Publication%20Files/Learning%20Through%20Noticing%20Field%202014_c1214d62-9429-4357-b47a-4195dae00881.pdf), §IV.C, pp.1341–1342; footnote29; TablesII–V. Supports: Bounded evidence in PSY-K06: Authors consider labour costs, numeracy and perceptibility; farmers knew a summary was coming.
- [PSY-S05: Social Learning and Incentives for Experimentation and Communication](https://spinup-000d1a-wp-offload-media.s3.amazonaws.com/faculty/wp-content/uploads/sites/45/2021/11/Benyishay_Mobarak_Malawi.pdf), §§2.1–2.2,5–7; Tables5–7. Supports: Bounded evidence in PSY-K07: Cross-randomised communicator/incentive design, knowledge exams and observed practices.
- [PSY-S06: Gender Gaps in Technology Diffusion (public author manuscript)](https://spinup-000d1a-wp-offload-media.s3.amazonaws.com/faculty/wp-content/uploads/sites/45/2019/10/GenderMalawi.pdf), Tables3–7, §§4.1–4.2.3, pp.14–21. Supports: Bounded evidence in PSY-K09: Objective competence, recipient ratings, training attendance, knowledge/adoption/yields measured separately.
- [PSY-S04: Can Network Theory-based Targeting Increase Technology Adoption? (January 2021 author manuscript)](https://faculty.wcas.northwestern.edu/~lab823/BBMM_Jan2021.pdf), §5 and pp.5–6; individual two-seed link analysis. Supports: Bounded evidence in PSY-K10: Two-seed connections predict learning/adoption under randomised seed assignments; author explicitly notes alternatives.
- [PSY-S09: The Stigma of Hearing Loss](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2904535/), Methods; Results: altered self-perception, ageism/vanity and supportive/nonsupportive others. Supports: Bounded evidence in PSY-K13: Separate repeated interviews elicited meanings and reasons before/after choices, including contrary accounts.
- [PSY-S11: Wheels of Change: Transforming Girls’ Lives with Bicycles (7 May 2025 author manuscript)](https://www.nishithprakash.com/s/Wheels_of_Change.pdf), Tables1/3 pp.38/40; §6.3 pp.20–21. Supports: Bounded evidence in PSY-K19: Formal fee/free equality p-values: commute .40; aggregate empowerment .13; agency subscales .11–.99.
- [PSY-S11: Wheels of Change: Transforming Girls’ Lives with Bicycles (7 May 2025 author manuscript)](https://www.nishithprakash.com/s/Wheels_of_Change.pdf), Table3 p.40; TableA.11 p.56; §6.3; §4.4.3 p.16. Supports: Bounded evidence in PSY-K20: Main locus-control0.18SD/bargaining0.21SD; controlled appendix0.16/0.19 with corrected p-values .02/.04 and <=.01. Pooled aspirations0.07(SE0.05) in Table3 and0.06(SE0.05), corrected p=.44/.50 in TableA.11. Behavioural games discontinued.
- [PSY-S14: Wheels of Change: Transforming Girls’ Lives with Bicycles (final journal abstract and metadata)](https://doi.org/10.1093/ej/ueaf122), Final journal abstract, aspirations sentence. Supports: Bounded evidence in PSY-K20: States that educational aspirations improved; final tables/outcome definitions and pooled-versus-arm-specific interpretation not inspected. This records the version discrepancy, not corroboration of a positive pooled effect.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** underdetermined
- **mechanism chain:** Intervention changes information, effort, resources or social setting -> observed behaviour; latent motive identification needs additional contrasts.
- **strongest rival:** Computation, credibility, delivery convenience, contact exposure, selection, service quality and material access can mimic an attributed psychological mechanism.
- **discriminating observation:** Measure beliefs/constraints before uptake, compare mechanism-specific arms directly, and distinguish a significant effect in one arm from a significant difference between arms.
- **evidence sought found:** Compared case-level claims PSY-K03, PSY-K06, PSY-K07, PSY-K09, PSY-K10, PSY-K13, PSY-K19, PSY-K20. Seaweed summaries bundle interpretation; Malawi gendered ratings do not yield a precise pooled adoption penalty; Zambia free/fee arms do not establish ownership effects; stigma interviews include dissenting participants.
- **remaining ambiguity:** Qualitative testimony is evidence of expressed meanings, not universal prevalence. Local package effects do not validate an invariant human motive.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis; targeted revision after independent first-round criticism

**Counterevidence/rival:** Computation, credibility, delivery convenience, contact exposure, selection, service quality and material access can mimic an attributed psychological mechanism.

**Inference limit:** Qualitative testimony is evidence of expressed meanings, not universal prevalence. Local package effects do not validate an invariant human motive.

## SYN-K21

Replication and dependable operation involve distinct material, measurement and learning arrangements. Documented laser copying, maintained gauges and navigator apprenticeship support bounded contributions. Failed visits limit contact sufficiency; successful rough electrodes limit strict-precision necessity. Universal contact necessity or precision sufficiency is not established by those observations.

**Cases:** OCE-C01; SKL-C01; SKL-C02; SKL-C03; SKL-C04; SKL-C06. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** hypothesis.

- [SKL-S01: K1 — Marine timekeeper / watch (ZAA0038), Board of Longitude Explorers](https://www.rmg.co.uk/sites/default/files/import/media/pdf/K1.pdf), Description. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K01: 1766 commission, 1769 completion and second Cook voyage use.
- [SKL-S02: Larcum Kendall — obituary, Gentleman's Magazine 60(2), pp. 1213–1214 (1790), transcribed in Cook's Log 40(2), p. 42 (2017)](https://www.captaincooksociety.com/cooks-life/people/cooks-officers-and-crew-and-contemporaries/larcum-kendall-obituary), Opening copying-contract paragraph. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K01: Contemporary account presents the commission as a test of another maker's execution.
- [SKL-S04: Hisashige Tanaka and His Myriad Year Clock](https://utcp.c.u-tokyo.ac.jp/publications/pdf/CollectionUTCP6_Hashimoto_02.pdf), pp. 36–37. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K04: Explains alternating foliots, marker adjustment and two automatic exceptions.
- [SKL-S05: What is a Traditional Japanese Clock (Wadokei)?](https://museum.seiko.co.jp/en/knowledge/relation_15/), Opening explanation. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K04: Defines seasonal-hour system and recurring adjustment.
- [SKL-S06: Archeological Investigation of the Armory Street, Lower Armory Grounds, Harpers Ferry Armory (46JF518)](https://npshistory.com/series/archeology/ncr/21/report.pdf), p. 59, notes 244–245. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K08: 1823 gauge adoption and separate gauges to check working gauges.
- [SKL-S12: Box of U.S. Rifle Model 1841 Inspection Gauges (View 4), HAFE 7427](https://artsandculture.google.com/asset/view-4/KAFQGvXgiHqy_Q?childAssetId=XgHi054cXIxrog), Catalog HAFE7427, ca.1846. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K08: Surviving Model 1841 inspection set attributed to Thornton; same set as S06 figure 19.
- [SKL-S08: African Motors: Technology, Gender, and the History of Development](https://www.bibliopen.org/p/bopen/9781478094456), pp. 136–142, table 2.1. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K12: Regional machine access, hand refinishing, contested quality, modular reliability, manuals and specialisation.
- [SKL-S10: The TEA Set: Tacit Knowledge and Scientific Networks](https://research.engineering.nyu.edu/~jbain/scitechsoc/readings/74Collins.pdf), pp. 172–173,176–177,182–183. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K16: Network scope/search checks, successes/failures, contact routes and explicit generalisation caveat.
- [SKL-S10: The TEA Set: Tacit Knowledge and Scientific Networks](https://research.engineering.nyu.edu/~jbain/scitechsoc/readings/74Collins.pdf), p. 177. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K17: Contrasts insuperable machining requirements with two reported successful rougher alternatives.
- [SKL-S11: Building a TEA Laser: The Caprices of Communication](https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/030631277500500404), Footnotes2,8,10–11. Supports: Bounded evidence in SKL-K18: Two variables changed together; teaching burden; at least six power-unit failures byJune1975; available written advice was not always consulted.
- [OCE-S01: Hokule‘a 1980 (reprinted in “A Journey by the Stars”)](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/GPO-CRECB-1980-pt20/pdf/GPO-CRECB-1980-pt20-1-3.pdf), p.25908, Hokule‘a 1980. Supports: Bounded evidence in OCE-K01: Training, successful landfall and return, twice-daily position reports and instrument comparison.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** underdetermined
- **mechanism chain:** Tools/references, practice and communication -> potentially competent replication/operation; product design and testing can substitute for some skill demands.
- **strongest rival:** Material differences, power-unit failures, instrumentation, selection of attempts and design robustness may explain success attributed to tacit transmission or precision.
- **discriminating observation:** Record all attempted builds and changed variables; compare instruction/contact, tools/materials and independent performance tests, including failed contacts and successful rough components.
- **evidence sought found:** Compared case-level claims SKL-K01, SKL-K04, SKL-K08, SKL-K12, SKL-K16, SKL-K17, SKL-K18, OCE-K01. Collins reports failed visits as well as contact-associated copies and does not rule out written-only success. Rough TEA electrodes limit strict-precision necessity across the reported configurations; they do not show that the blocked laboratory could succeed with the same substitution. K1 is one replication, not serial manufacture.
- **remaining ambiguity:** Small historical series and partial 1975 access prevent exclusive causal attribution; successful operation does not establish reliability, safety or transferable livelihoods.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis; targeted revision after independent first-round criticism

**Counterevidence/rival:** Material differences, power-unit failures, instrumentation, selection of attempts and design robustness may explain success attributed to tacit transmission or precision.

**Inference limit:** Small historical series and partial 1975 access prevent exclusive causal attribution; successful operation does not establish reliability, safety or transferable livelihoods.

## SYN-K22

Institutions can preserve selected uses, alter obligations and distribute gains or exposure differently: Tokugawa gun niches, Yuan exchange practices, Qing connected-market risk, metric compromises and negotiated Tae Rak water levels do not have one common welfare or adoption sign.

**Cases:** OCE-C02; OCE-C03; POL-C01; POL-C02; POL-C03; POL-C04. **Evidence:** synthesis-inference. **Confidence:** medium. **Causal status:** descriptive.

- [POL-S01: Giving Up the Gun? Overcoming Myths about Japanese Sword-Hunting and Firearms Control](https://www.isc.meiji.ac.jp/~transfer/papers/en/pdf/06/04_Enomoto.pdf), II, pp. 50–53. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K02: Local firearm records and distinctions among possession, carriage and use.
- [POL-S02: The Social Life of Firearms in Tokugawa Japan](https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/10371390902780530?journalCode=cjst20), Abstract. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K02: Common rural firearms and farm-tool uses.
- [POL-S03: Introduction: the reception of the harquebus in East and South-East Asia](https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/international-journal-of-asian-studies/article/introduction-the-reception-of-the-harquebus-in-east-and-southeast-asia/AD08FBBF2D32EDBC28B3AE2D7200CBCF), Japan section, p. 9. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K02: Continued elite and non-elite peacetime niches.
- [POL-S05: The distinctive uses of two monetary units, guanwen and dingliang, in Yuan China (1260–1368) as recorded in 'Heicheng chutuwenshu' and 'Huizhou qiyuewenshu'](https://www.jstage.jst.go.jp/article/sehs/68/3/68_KJ00004582583/_article/-char/en), English abstract. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K08: Regional account-unit and government/commercial distinctions.
- [POL-S04: The rise and fall of paper money in Yuan China, 1260–1368](https://www.hoover.org/sites/default/files/2025-01/Rise%20and%20Demise%20of%20Paper%20Money%20in%20Yuan%20China.pdf), p. 15, Table 2; p. 13 note 22. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K08: Private silver use and Yunnan cowry exception.
- [POL-S07: Communication Infrastructure and Stabilizing Food Prices: Evidence from the Telegraph Network in China](https://assets.aeaweb.org/asset-server/files/13141.pdf), sections IV–V, pp. 22–29. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K14: Weather-location interactions, disconnected-region placebo and volatility heterogeneity.
- [POL-S10: Décret concernant l’uniformité des poids et mesures, 12 février 1812](https://annales.ensmp.fr/articles/1812-1/235-245.pdf), printed pp. 453–454, articles 1–6. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K15: Direct decree on unchanged units, adapted instruments and teaching.
- [POL-S11: Les origines du système métrique en France et la Convention du mètre de 1875, qui a ouvert la voie au Système international d’unités et à sa révision de 2018](https://comptes-rendus.academie-sciences.fr/physique/item/10.1016/j.crhy.2018.12.002.pdf), pp. 13–14. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K15: Retail implementation and legal continuity.
- [POL-S11: Les origines du système métrique en France et la Convention du mètre de 1875, qui a ouvert la voie au Système international d’unités et à sa révision de 2018](https://comptes-rendus.academie-sciences.fr/physique/item/10.1016/j.crhy.2018.12.002.pdf), pp. 13–14, quoted articles 1 and 3. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K17: Repeal and effective date.
- [POL-S13: A History of the Metric System Controversy in the United States](https://nvlpubs.nist.gov/nistpubs/Legacy/SP/nbsspecialpublication345-10.pdf), pp. 10–11, note 53. Supports: Bounded evidence in POL-K17: Translated prohibitions including commercial/legal settings.
- [OCE-S05: Budj Bim Cultural Landscape: World Heritage Nomination](https://whc.unesco.org/document/166283), pp.68–69. Supports: Bounded evidence in OCE-K06: Opposition, drainage, short water retention and traps rarely functioning outside floods.
- [OCE-S06: Lake Condah Restoration Project](https://www.gunditjmirring.com/lake-condah-restoration-project), opening project-history paragraphs. Supports: Bounded evidence in OCE-K06: Drain completion in 1954 and later restoration attempts.
- [OCE-S05: Budj Bim Cultural Landscape: World Heritage Nomination](https://whc.unesco.org/document/166283), pp.69–70. Supports: Bounded evidence in OCE-K07: 2010 restoration, selected water level and differing trap reach.
- [OCE-S06: Lake Condah Restoration Project](https://www.gunditjmirring.com/lake-condah-restoration-project), 2004–2010 project sequence. Supports: Bounded evidence in OCE-K07: Failed early trial, planning, hydrological studies, land return and construction.
- [OCE-S08: Aboriginal hunting buffers climate-driven fire-size variability in Australia’s spinifex grasslands](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC3387077/), Materials and Methods, paragraphs on social/economic disincentives and kanyinin/Jukurrpa. Supports: Bounded evidence in OCE-K20: Ethnographic account of rights, costs, responsibilities and public-good recognition.

**Diagnostic test:**

- **status:** underdetermined
- **mechanism chain:** Task-specific permissions, enforcement and access arrangements -> heterogeneous feasible use and distribution; no single institutional coefficient is inferred.
- **strongest rival:** Prices, ecology, security demand and material service constraints change alongside legal form, so law alone need not explain uptake.
- **discriminating observation:** Compare actual access/use and affected actors before and after specified arrangements; retain household practice, excluded sites and redistributed risk alongside legal deadlines or mean benefits.
- **evidence sought found:** Compared case-level claims POL-K02, POL-K08, POL-K14, POL-K15, POL-K17, OCE-K06, OCE-K07, OCE-K20. 1840 is a legal deadline, not universal uptake; telegraph access reduces price maxima but redistributes weather exposure; the 2010 water setting permits partial restoration with a named exclusion.
- **remaining ambiguity:** Heterogeneous histories are analogical comparisons, not a pooled policy evaluation. Administrative records and abstract-only sources retain their stated limits.
- **hypothesis timing:** retrospective synthesis; targeted revision after independent first-round criticism

**Counterevidence/rival:** Prices, ecology, security demand and material service constraints change alongside legal form, so law alone need not explain uptake.

**Inference limit:** Heterogeneous histories are analogical comparisons, not a pooled policy evaluation. Administrative records and abstract-only sources retain their stated limits.
